import React, { useState } from 'react'
import { useLang } from './LangContext'
import { photos, photoOrder } from './constants'

export const PhotoGallery = ({ posts = [], onOpenPhoto, onOpenNotionPhoto }) => {
  const { t } = useLang()
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all')

  // Extract Notion posts tagged or categorized under '摄影' / 'Photo' / '写真'
  const notionPhotos = (posts || [])
    .filter(p => {
      const isPhotoCat =
        p.category === '摄影' ||
        p.category === 'Photo' ||
        p.category === '写真' ||
        p.category === 'Photos'
      const hasPhotoTag = p.tagItems?.some(tag =>
        ['摄影', 'Photo', '写真', '摄影集', 'Photos'].includes(tag.name)
      )
      const hasImg = !!(p.pageCover || p.pageCoverThumbnail)
      return (isPhotoCat || hasPhotoTag) && hasImg
    })
    .map(p => ({
      id: p.id,
      isNotion: true,
      title: p.title || 'Untitled Photo',
      place: p.summary || 'Notion 随拍',
      thumb: p.pageCoverThumbnail || p.pageCover,
      full: p.pageCover || p.pageCoverThumbnail,
      date: p.date?.start_date || p.publishDay || p.publishDate || '',
      category: p.tagItems?.some(tag =>
        ['自然', '风景', 'landscape'].includes(tag.name)
      )
        ? 'landscape'
        : 'everyday',
      slug: p.slug || p.id
    }))

  // Deduplicate: If a photo title exists in Notion, supersede the local one
  const notionTitles = new Set(notionPhotos.map(p => p.title.trim()))

  const remainingLocalPhotos = photos.filter((p, idx) => {
    const localTitle = t('photoTitle' + idx)?.trim()
    return !notionTitles.has(localTitle)
  })

  const allPhotosCount = remainingLocalPhotos.length + notionPhotos.length
  const landscapeCount =
    remainingLocalPhotos.filter(p => p.category === 'landscape').length +
    notionPhotos.filter(p => p.category === 'landscape').length
  const everydayCount =
    remainingLocalPhotos.filter(p => p.category === 'everyday').length +
    notionPhotos.filter(p => p.category === 'everyday').length

  const filteredNotionPhotos = notionPhotos.filter(p => {
    if (filter === 'all') return true
    return p.category === filter
  })

  const filteredLocalOrder = photoOrder.filter(idx => {
    const localTitle = t('photoTitle' + idx)?.trim()
    if (notionTitles.has(localTitle)) return false
    if (filter === 'all') return true
    return photos[idx]?.category === filter
  })

  return (
    <section className='gallery wrap' id='photos' aria-labelledby='photos-title'>
      <div className='section-heading'>
        <div>
          <span className='eyebrow mono'>04 / AWAY FROM THE KEYBOARD</span>
          <h2 id='photos-title'>{t('photosTitle')}</h2>
        </div>
        <span className='margin-note'>{t('photosNote')}</span>
      </div>

      <div className='gallery-toolbar'>
        <p className='mono'>{t('photosIntro')}</p>
        <div className='filters' role='toolbar' aria-label='Filter photos'>
          <button
            type='button'
            className={filter === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}
            aria-pressed={filter === 'all'}
            onClick={() => setFilter('all')}>
            {t('filterAll') || `全部 / ${allPhotosCount}`}
          </button>
          <button
            type='button'
            className={filter === 'landscape' ? 'active' : ''}
            aria-pressed={filter === 'landscape'}
            onClick={() => setFilter('landscape')}>
            {t('filterLandscape') || `自然 / 0${landscapeCount}`}
          </button>
          <button
            type='button'
            className={filter === 'everyday' ? 'active' : ''}
            aria-pressed={filter === 'everyday'}
            onClick={() => setFilter('everyday')}>
            {t('filterEveryday') || `光与日常 / ${everydayCount}`}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className='timeline'>
        <div
          className='timeline-photos'
          style={{
            display: 'grid',
            gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr))',
            gap: '24px',
            marginTop: '28px'
          }}>
          {/* Notion Dynamic Photos Stream */}
          {filteredNotionPhotos.map((photo, seq) => (
            <button
              key={photo.id}
              type='button'
              className='photo-card'
              style={{
                background: '#fffef8',
                border: 'var(--border)',
                boxShadow: '4px 5px var(--ink)',
                padding: '10px 10px 12px',
                textAlign: 'left',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column'
              }}
              onClick={() => onOpenNotionPhoto && onOpenNotionPhoto(photo)}
              aria-label={photo.title}>
              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  marginBottom: '6px'
                }}>
                <span
                  className='card-number mono'
                  style={{ fontSize: '10px' }}>
                  {`NEW / 0${seq + 1}`}
                </span>
                <span
                  className='mono'
                  style={{
                    fontSize: '9px',
                    background: 'var(--green)',
                    padding: '1px 5px',
                    border: '1px solid var(--ink)',
                    fontWeight: 700
                  }}>
                  NOTION
                </span>
              </div>
              <div
                className='photo-crop'
                style={{
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  border: '1px solid var(--ink)',
                  marginBottom: '8px'
                }}>
                <img
                  src={photo.thumb}
                  alt={photo.title}
                  loading='lazy'
                  style={{ width: '100%', height: '220px', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                />
              </div>
              <div
                className='gallery-caption'
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  alignItems: 'flex-start'
                }}>
                <div>
                  <strong style={{ fontSize: '14px', display: 'block' }}>
                    {photo.title}
                  </strong>
                  <small style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
                    {photo.place}
                  </small>
                </div>
                <span className='mono' style={{ fontSize: '10px' }}>
                  {photo.date} ↗
                </span>
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}

          {/* Local Curated 17 Photos */}
          {filteredLocalOrder.map((photoIdx, seq) => {
            const photo = photos[photoIdx]
            const imgSrc = `/themes/field-notes/assets/${photo.file}-small.jpg`
            const title = t('photoTitle' + photoIdx)
            const place = t(photo.place)
            const globalSeq = filteredNotionPhotos.length + seq + 1

            return (
              <button
                key={photo.file}
                type='button'
                className='photo-card'
                style={{
                  background: '#fffef8',
                  border: 'var(--border)',
                  boxShadow: '4px 5px var(--ink)',
                  padding: '10px 10px 12px',
                  textAlign: 'left',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column'
                }}
                onClick={() => onOpenPhoto && onOpenPhoto(photoIdx)}
                aria-label={title}>
                <span
                  className='card-number mono'
                  style={{ fontSize: '10px', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                  {String(globalSeq).padStart(2, '0')}
                </span>
                <div
                  className='photo-crop'
                  style={{
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    border: '1px solid var(--ink)',
                    marginBottom: '8px'
                  }}>
                  <img
                    src={imgSrc}
                    alt={t('photoAlt' + photoIdx)}
                    loading='lazy'
                    style={{ width: '100%', height: '220px', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div
                  className='gallery-caption'
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'flex-start'
                  }}>
                  <div>
                    <strong style={{ fontSize: '14px', display: 'block' }}>
                      {title}
                    </strong>
                    <small style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
                      {place}
                    </small>
                  </div>
                  <span className='mono' style={{ fontSize: '10px' }}>
                    {photo.date} ↗
                  </span>
                </div>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className='gallery-end mono' style={{ marginTop: '40px' }}>
        <span>FUJIFILM X-S20 / 33mm</span>
        <span>{t('galleryEnd')}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}



================================================================================
FILE: themes/field-notes/components/Lightbox.js
DESCRIPTION: themes/field-notes/components/Lightbox.js (Fullscreen Polaroid Modal with EXIF Display)
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useLang } from './LangContext'
import { heroPhotos } from './constants'

export const Lightbox = ({
  viewerState,
  onClose,
  onStep,
  photoList = [],
  projectList = []
}) => {
  const { t } = useLang()
  const dialogRef = useRef(null)
  const imgRef = useRef(null)

  const { open, mode, index, screen, projectIndex } = viewerState

  const updateViewerRatio = useCallback(() => {
    const dialog = dialogRef.current
    const img = imgRef.current
    if (dialog && img && img.naturalWidth && img.naturalHeight) {
      const ratio = img.naturalWidth / img.naturalHeight
      dialog.style.setProperty('--viewer-ratio', String(ratio))
      dialog.setAttribute(
        'data-orientation',
        ratio < 0.95 ? 'portrait' : 'landscape'
      )
      dialog.scrollTop = 0
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    const dialog = dialogRef.current
    if (!dialog) return
    if (open && !dialog.open) {
      try {
        dialog.showModal()
      } catch (e) {
        dialog.setAttribute('open', '')
      }
      if (imgRef.current && imgRef.current.complete) {
        updateViewerRatio()
      }
    } else if (!open && dialog.open) {
      dialog.close()
    }
  }, [open, index, mode, screen, projectIndex, updateViewerRatio])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    const handleKeyDown = e => {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault()
        onStep(-1)
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault()
        onStep(1)
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
  }, [open, onStep])

  if (!open) return null

  let imgSrc = ''
  let alt = ''
  let title = ''
  let indexText = ''
  let caption = ''
  let articleLink = null

  if (mode === 'photo') {
    const photo = photoList[index]
    if (photo) {
      imgSrc = photo.fullSrc || photo.thumbSrc || ''
      alt = photo.alt || photo.title || ''
      title = photo.title || ''
      indexText = `FIELD NOTES / ${String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} — ${String(photoList.length).padStart(2, '0')}`
      const desc = photo.description ? `${photo.description}\n` : ''
      caption =
        desc +
        [photo.place, photo.date, photo.camera, photo.settings]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(' · ')
      if (photo.isNotion && (photo.href || photo.slug)) {
        articleLink = photo.href || `/${photo.slug}`
      }
    }
  } else if (mode === 'hero') {
    const photo = heroPhotos[index]
    if (photo) {
      imgSrc = `/themes/field-notes/assets/${photo.file}.jpg`
      alt = t(photo.key + 'Alt')
      title = t(photo.key + 'Title')
      indexText = `OFFLINE MOMENTS / 0${index + 1} — 02`
      caption =
        t(photo.key + 'Desc') +
        '\n' +
        [t(photo.key + 'Place'), photo.date, photo.camera]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(' · ')
    }
  } else {
    const proj = projectList[projectIndex || 0]
    if (proj) {
      const screenIdx =
        typeof screen === 'number'
          ? screen
          : screen === 'admin'
          ? 1
          : 0
      imgSrc = proj.images
        ? proj.images[screenIdx] || proj.images[0]
        : `/themes/field-notes/assets/${proj.files ? proj.files[screenIdx] : 'r2-share'}.jpg`
      alt = (proj.alts && proj.alts[screenIdx]) || proj.name
      title = `${proj.name} / ${(proj.screens && proj.screens[screenIdx]) || 'SCREEN'}`
      indexText = `SELECTED PROJECT / ${String((projectIndex || 0) + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`
      caption = proj.caption || proj.desc || ''
      if (proj.url) {
        articleLink = proj.url
      }
    }
  }

  const showNav = mode === 'photo' || mode === 'hero'
  const isExtLink = articleLink && /^https?:\/\//i.test(String(articleLink))

  return (
    <dialog
      ref={dialogRef}
      id='viewer'
      aria-labelledby='viewer-title'
      onClose={onClose}
      onClick={e => {
        if (e.target === dialogRef.current) {
          const r = dialogRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()
          if (
            e.clientX < r.left ||
            e.clientX > r.right ||
            e.clientY < r.top ||
            e.clientY > r.bottom
          ) {
            onClose()
          }
        }
      }}
    >
      <button
        id='close-viewer'
        type='button'
        aria-label={t('close')}
        onClick={onClose}
      >
        ×
      </button>

      <div className='viewer-image-wrap'>
        <img
          ref={imgRef}
          id='viewer-image'
          src={imgSrc}
          alt={alt}
          onLoad={updateViewerRatio}
        />
      </div>

      <div className='viewer-caption-bar'>
        <div className='viewer-title-row'>
          <h2 id='viewer-title'>{title}</h2>
          {articleLink && (
            <div className='viewer-action'>
              {isExtLink ? (
                <a
                  href={articleLink}
                  className='button ink viewer-link-btn'
                  onClick={onClose}
                  target='_blank'
                  rel='noopener noreferrer'
                >
                  <span>{t('viewProjectLink')}</span>
                </a>
              ) : (
                <Link
                  href={articleLink}
                  className='button ink viewer-link-btn'
                  onClick={onClose}
                >
                  <span>{t('viewNoteLink')}</span>
                </Link>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <p id='viewer-caption'>{caption}</p>
      </div>

      <div className='viewer-footer'>
        {showNav ? (
          <button
            type='button'
            id='prev-photo'
            aria-label={t('prev')}
            onClick={() => onStep(-1)}
          >
            ←
          </button>
        ) : (
          <span />
        )}
        <span className='mono' id='viewer-index'>
          {indexText}
        </span>
        {showNav ? (
          <button
            type='button'
            id='next-photo'
            aria-label={t('next')}
            onClick={() => onStep(1)}
          >
            →
          </button>
        ) : (
          <span />
        )}
      </div>
    </dialog>
  )
}



================================================================================
FILE: themes/field-notes/components/ArticleSection.js
DESCRIPTION: themes/field-notes/components/ArticleSection.js (Curated Article Feed & Category Filter)
================================================================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useLang } from './LangContext'
import { localizeCategory, localizeTag } from '../data'

export const ArticleSection = ({ posts = [] }) => {
  const { lang, t } = useLang()
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('all')

  const categories = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map()
    for (const p of posts) {
      const rawKey = p.category || '随笔'
      const label = p.localizedCategory || localizeCategory(rawKey, lang)
      const prev = map.get(rawKey)
      map.set(rawKey, { label, count: (prev?.count || 0) + 1 })
    }
    return Array.from(map.entries())
  }, [posts, lang])

  const filteredPosts = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeCategory === 'all') return posts
    return posts.filter(p => (p.category || '随笔') === activeCategory)
  }, [posts, activeCategory])

  const featuredPost = filteredPosts.length > 0 ? filteredPosts[0] : null
  const gridPosts = filteredPosts.length > 1 ? filteredPosts.slice(1) : []

  return (
    <section
      className='articles-section wrap'
      id='notes'
      aria-labelledby='articles-title'
    >
      <div id='articles' style={{ position: 'relative', top: '-20px' }}></div>
      <div className='section-heading'>
        <div>
          <span className='eyebrow mono'>02 / FIELD NOTES & WRITINGS</span>
          <h2 id='articles-title'>{t('notesTitle')}</h2>
        </div>
        <span className='margin-note'>{t('notesNote')}</span>
      </div>

      {/* 和纸胶带 / 分类筛选工具栏 */}
      {categories.length > 0 && (
        <div
          className='filters'
          style={{
            marginBottom: '28px',
            display: 'flex',
            flexWrap: 'wrap',
            gap: '10px'
          }}
        >
          <button
            type='button'
            className={`mono ${activeCategory === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveCategory('all')}
            style={{
              border: 'var(--border)',
              padding: '6px 14px',
              fontWeight: 700,
              background: activeCategory === 'all' ? 'var(--ink)' : 'var(--paper)',
              color: activeCategory === 'all' ? 'var(--paper)' : 'var(--ink)',
              boxShadow: activeCategory === 'all' ? '3px 3px var(--purple)' : '2px 2px var(--ink)',
              cursor: 'pointer'
            }}
          >
            {t('filterAll')?.split('/')[0] || '全部'} / {posts.length}
          </button>
          {categories.map(([catKey, { label, count }]) => (
            <button
              key={catKey}
              type='button'
              className={`mono ${activeCategory === catKey ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => setActiveCategory(catKey)}
              style={{
                border: 'var(--border)',
                padding: '6px 14px',
                fontWeight: 700,
                background: activeCategory === catKey ? 'var(--ink)' : 'var(--paper)',
                color: activeCategory === catKey ? 'var(--paper)' : 'var(--ink)',
                boxShadow: activeCategory === catKey ? '3px 3px var(--purple)' : '2px 2px var(--ink)',
                cursor: 'pointer'
              }}
            >
              {label} / {count}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {filteredPosts.length === 0 ? (
        <p className='mono' style={{ padding: '30px 0', color: 'var(--muted)' }}>
          {t('noPosts')}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Featured Field Note: Tactile Paper Layout */}
          {featuredPost && (
            <article className='featured-note-paper'>
              <div className='featured-note-copy'>
                <div className='featured-note-top mono'>
                  <span className='small-tag'>LATEST NOTE — 001</span>
                  {(featuredPost.localizedCategory || featuredPost.category) && (
                    <span className='article-card-category'>
                      {featuredPost.localizedCategory || featuredPost.category}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <span style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', fontSize: '11px', color: '#686960' }}>
                    {featuredPost.formattedDate ||
                      featuredPost?.date?.start_date ||
                      featuredPost?.publishDay ||
                      ''}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <Link href={featuredPost.href || `/${featuredPost.slug || featuredPost.id}`}>
                  <h3>
                    <span>{featuredPost.localizedTitle || featuredPost.title}</span>
                    <span aria-hidden='true'> ↗</span>
                  </h3>
                </Link>

                {(featuredPost.localizedSummary || featuredPost.summary) && (
                  <p className='featured-note-summary'>
                    {featuredPost.localizedSummary || featuredPost.summary}
                  </p>
                )}

                {featuredPost.displayTags && featuredPost.displayTags.length > 0 && (
                  <div className='note-memo-tags'>
                    {featuredPost.displayTags.map(tag => (
                      <span key={typeof tag === 'string' ? tag : tag.name} className='note-memo-tag'>
                        #{localizeTag(tag, lang)}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div style={{ marginTop: 'auto', paddingTop: '16px' }}>
                  <Link
                    href={featuredPost.href || `/${featuredPost.slug || featuredPost.id}`}
                    className='button ink'
                  >
                    <span>{t('readMore')}</span>
                    <span aria-hidden='true'>↗</span>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className='featured-note-visual'>
                <div className='window-bar'>
                  <span className='window-dots'>● ● ●</span>
                  <span className='mono'>FIELD NOTE / SNAPSHOT</span>
                  <span>↗</span>
                </div>
                <Link
                  href={featuredPost.href || `/${featuredPost.slug || featuredPost.id}`}
                  style={{ display: 'block', overflow: 'hidden' }}
                >
                  {featuredPost.pageCoverThumbnail || featuredPost.pageCover ? (
                    <img
                      className='featured-note-cover'
                      src={featuredPost.pageCoverThumbnail || featuredPost.pageCover}
                      alt={featuredPost.localizedTitle || featuredPost.title || ''}
                      loading='lazy'
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <div
                      className='featured-note-cover'
                      style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        fontSize: '48px',
                        background: '#f2f0e6'
                      }}
                    >
                      {featuredPost.pageIcon || '📝'}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </Link>
              </div>
            </article>
          )}

          {/* Grid of Note Memos with Washi Tape */}
          {gridPosts.length > 0 && (
            <div className='notes-memo-grid'>
              {gridPosts.map((post, idx) => {
                const postDate =
                  post.formattedDate ||
                  post?.date?.start_date ||
                  post?.publishDay ||
                  ''
                const href = post.href || `/${post.slug || post.id}`

                return (
                  <Link key={post.id || idx} href={href} className='note-memo-card'>
                    <div className='note-memo-tape' aria-hidden='true'></div>

                    <div className='note-memo-header mono'>
                      <span className='article-card-category'>
                        {post.localizedCategory || post.category || 'NOTE'}
                      </span>
                      <span
                        className='mono'
                        style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#686960' }}
                      >
                        {postDate}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {post.pageCoverThumbnail || post.pageCover ? (
                      <img
                        className='note-memo-cover'
                        src={post.pageCoverThumbnail || post.pageCover}
                        alt={post.localizedTitle || post.title || ''}
                        loading='lazy'
                      />
                    ) : null}

                    <h3 className='note-memo-title'>
                      {post.localizedTitle || post.title}
                    </h3>

                    {(post.localizedSummary || post.summary) && (
                      <p className='note-memo-desc'>
                        {post.localizedSummary || post.summary}
                      </p>
                    )}

                    {post.displayTags && post.displayTags.length > 0 && (
                      <div className='note-memo-tags'>
                        {post.displayTags.map(tag => (
                          <span
                            key={typeof tag === 'string' ? tag : tag.name}
                            className='note-memo-tag'
                          >
                            #{localizeTag(tag, lang)}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className='note-memo-foot mono'>
                      <span>{`NOTE #${String(idx + 2).padStart(2, '0')}`}</span>
                      <span className='note-memo-arrow'>
                        <span>{t('readMore')}</span>
                        <span>↗</span>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className='article-action-bar'>
            <Link href='/archive' className='button ink'>
              <span>{t('allNotes')}</span>
              <span aria-hidden='true'>↗</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

const DOODLES = {
  arrow: (
    <svg viewBox='0 0 48 32' aria-hidden='true'>
      <path d='M3 24c10-18 26-20 38-8M33 10l9 6-11 4' />
    </svg>
  ),

      <path d='M16 3v26M3 16h26M7 7l18 18M25 7 7 25' />
    </svg>
  )

const Piece = ({ it }) => {
  const style = { '--r': `${it.r || 0}deg`, '--s': it.s || 1, '--y': `${it.y || 0}px` }
  const minor = it.minor ? ' is-minor' : ''
  if (it.t === 'note') {
    return (
      <li className={`lab-note${minor}`} style={{ ...style, backgroundColor: it.color || '#fff3a8' }}>
        {it.text}
      </li>
    )
  }
  if (it.t === 'tape') {
    return (
      <li className={`lab-tape lab-tape-${it.theme || 'blue'}${minor}`} style={style}>
        {it.text}
      </li>
    )
  }
  if (it.t === 'doodle') {
    return <li className={`lab-doodle${minor}`} style={style}>{DOODLES[it.id]}</li>
  }
  if (it.t === 'stamp') {
    return (
      <li className={`lab-stamp lab-stamp-${it.kind || 'red'}${minor}`} style={style}>
        <span>{it.text}</span>
        <b>{it.big}</b>
        <small>{it.sub}</small>
      </li>
    )
  }
  return (
    <li className={`lab-sticker${minor}`} data-name={it.name} style={style}>
      {ICONS[it.id]}
    </li>
  )
}

export const StackStickers = ({ variant = 'art' }) => {
  const { lang, t } = useLang()

  const notePort =
    lang === 'ja'
      ? 'ポート開放ゼロ'
      : lang === 'en'
      ? 'Zero open ports'
      : '不开路由器端口'

  const noteRun =
    lang === 'ja'
      ? '静かに動く。'
      : lang === 'en'
      ? 'it just runs.'
      : '让服务自己跑。'

  const noteDual =
    lang === 'ja'
      ? 'デュアル LAN・帯域分離'
      : lang === 'en'
      ? 'Dual LAN: dedicated line'
      : '双网口直连 · 互不抢带宽'

  const noteWatch =
    lang === 'ja'
      ? '10分ごとのエッジ死活監視'
      : lang === 'en'
      ? '10-min Edge watchdog'
      : '10m 级边缘看门狗'

  const noteHash =
    lang === 'ja'
      ? 'ハッシュ整合性検証'
      : lang === 'en'
      ? 'SHA-256 verified'
      : 'SHA-256 逐片验签'

  const noteHours = '08:00 · 16:30 · 21:30'

  const title = t('labStackTitle') || 'STACK · 容器、网络与云原生底座'

  // 贴纸墙：精心编排的 7 簇错落贴纸。既保持有机手工手账的拼贴层次感，又确保桌面与手机双端饱满匀称，告别单侧空白与过度拉伸
  const WALL = [
    // 01 / 云原生入口与零信任接入
    { align: 'spread', ind: '1%', items: [
      { t: 'icon', id: 'docker', name: 'Docker Engine', r: -6, s: 1.35, y: 0 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'cloudflare', name: 'Cloudflare Zero Trust', r: 5, s: 1.2, y: 5 },
      { t: 'note', text: notePort, r: -3, color: '#fff3a8', y: -2 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'tailscale', name: 'Tailscale Mesh', r: 7, s: 0.95, y: -2 },
      { t: 'doodle', id: 'star', r: 12, minor: 1 }
    ] },

    // 02 / 端口印章、调度与内网防护
    { align: 'spread', ind: '0%', items: [
      { t: 'stamp', kind: 'red', text: 'NO OPEN', big: '0', sub: 'PORTS', r: -11, y: 1 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'k8s', name: 'Kubernetes', r: -7, s: 1.15, y: 3 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'adguard', name: 'AdGuard Home', r: 5, s: 1.05, y: -5 },
      { t: 'tape', text: '$ ssh home', r: -3, theme: 'blue', y: 0 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'syncthing', name: 'Syncthing Photo Sync', r: 6, s: 0.95, y: 4 }
    ] },

    // 03 / 私有存储中心与物理隔离专用线
    { align: 'spread', ind: '2%', items: [
      { t: 'icon', id: 'nas', name: 'Synology DS720+', r: 6, s: 1.3, y: 2 },
      { t: 'note', text: noteDual, r: -2, color: '#ffe4ed', y: -3 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'cli', name: 'Linux CLI VM', r: -5, s: 1.1, y: 5 },
      { t: 'stamp', kind: 'green', text: '100% HEALTHY', big: 'OK', sub: 'DAILY AUDIT', r: 10, y: -3, minor: 1 }
    ] },

    // 04 / 动态中继、脚本看护与告警推送
    { align: 'spread', ind: '0%', items: [
      { t: 'doodle', id: 'arrow', r: -8, minor: 1 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'relay', name: '64G Motion Photo Relay', r: -4, s: 1.25, y: 3 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'python', name: 'Python Automations', r: 5, s: 1.05, y: -5 },
      { t: 'note', text: noteWatch, r: -2, color: '#d2fc4b', y: 2 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'telegram', name: 'Telegram Bot Alert', r: 8, s: 1.05, y: -2 }
    ] },

    // 05 / 滚动缓冲与全家桶备份矩阵
    { align: 'spread', ind: '1%', items: [
      { t: 'tape', text: '64 GiB ROLLING BUFFER', r: -4, theme: 'yellow', y: 3, minor: 1 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'googlephotos', name: 'Google Photos Backup', r: -7, s: 1.05, y: 1 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'apple', name: 'Apple Time Machine', r: 5, s: 1.15, y: -4 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'github', name: 'GitHub Actions / CLI', r: -5, s: 1.05, y: -2 },
      { t: 'stamp', kind: 'blue', text: 'ZERO TRUST', big: 'EDGE', sub: 'TUNNEL NRT', r: -9, y: 3 }
    ] },

    // 06 / 边缘 Serverless、签名校验与媒体底座
    { align: 'spread', ind: '0%', items: [
      { t: 'note', text: noteHash, r: 3, color: '#dcfce7', y: 3 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'workers', name: 'Cloudflare Workers & D1', r: 6, s: 1.18, y: -3 },
      { t: 'tape', text: '$ docker compose ps', r: -2, theme: 'purple', y: 0, minor: 1 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'jellyfin', name: 'Jellyfin Media', r: -5, s: 1.05, y: 4 },
      { t: 'icon', id: 'ubuntu', name: 'Ubuntu 24.04 LTS', r: 7, s: 1.0, y: -2 }
    ] },

    // 07 / 自动化作息巡航与收尾印信
    { align: 'center', ind: '0%', items: [
      { t: 'note', text: noteHours, r: -3, color: '#f3e8ff', y: 3, minor: 1 },
      { t: 'note', text: noteRun, r: 3, color: '#fffef8', y: -3, s: 1.05 },
      { t: 'doodle', id: 'star', r: -15, minor: 1 }
    ] }
  ]

  return (
    <div
      className={`lab-stack-board ${variant === 'inline' ? 'lab-stack-inline' : 'lab-stack-art'}`}
      aria-hidden='true'
    >
      <span className='lab-stack-tag'>{title}</span>
      <div className='lab-stack-wall'>
        {WALL.map((row, ri) => (
          <ul key={ri} className={`lab-row is-${row.align}`} style={{ '--ind': row.ind }}>
            {row.items.map((it, i) => (
              <Piece key={i} it={it} />
            ))}
          </ul>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
export default StackStickers

DESCRIPTION: themes/field-notes/components/Footer.js (Colophon & Minimal Footer)

import React from 'react'
import { useLang } from './LangContext'

export const Closing = () => {
  const { t } = useLang()

  const scrollToTop = () => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className='closing wrap'>
      <span className='closing-star' aria-hidden='true'>
        ✳
      </span>
      <div>
        <span className='eyebrow mono'>THE NOTEBOOK STAYS OPEN.</span>
        <h2>{t('closing')}</h2>
        <p>{t('closingDesc')}</p>
      </div>
      <button
        type='button'
        className='back-top'
        onClick={scrollToTop}
        aria-label={t('backTop')}>
        ↑
      </button>
    </section>
  )
}

export const Footer = () => {
  return (
    <footer className='wrap'>
      <span>© 2026 Developer</span>
      <span className='mono'>MADE OF CODE & EVERYDAY LIFE.</span>
      <a
        href='https://github.com/example-dev'
        target='_blank'
        rel='noopener noreferrer'>
        GitHub ↗
      </a>
    </footer>
  )
}
